## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-22

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　edit_mode_for_named_version

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 指定版本的編輯模式 （可選）
- 指定將用于指定版本上的子網更新的編輯模式。 注: 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 4 或更高，并且僅適用于企業級地理資料庫。 無事件—事件將不會用于指定版本上的子網更新。 對于在此版本中編輯的圖徵和對象，此編輯模式將更新其子網名稱和傳播值。 這是默認設置。 具有事件—事件將用于指定版本上的子網更新。 當子網更新時，此編輯模式將應用地理資料庫行為（例如，屬性規則、編輯者追蹤等），并為所有適用的圖徵和對象更新子網名稱和傳播值。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-27

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　manage_subnetwork_isdirty

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 管理 IsDirty （可選）
- 指定子網表中的 Is dirty 屬性是否將由更新子網操作進行管理。 默認情況下，如果沒有為層定義任何子網控制器，則將使用 NOT_MANAGE 選項。 注: 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 選中—Is dirty 屬性將由更新子網操作管理。 除非另有說明，否則此為默認設置。 未選中—Is dirty 屬性不會由更新子網操作管理。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-28

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　include_containers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 包括容器 （可選）
- 指定是否在追蹤結果中包含容器圖徵和對象。 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 選中—將在追蹤結果中包含容器圖徵和對象。 當與 update_structure_features 和 update_container_features 參數配合使用時，容器圖徵的受支持子網名稱欄位將更新為其支持的子網的子網名稱。 未選中—不會在追蹤結果中包含容器圖徵和對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-29

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　include_content

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 包括內容 （可選）
- 指定追蹤是否將在結果中包含容器的內容。 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 選中—將在追蹤結果中包含容器圖徵的內容。 在更新子網操作期間，將更新追蹤返回的內容圖徵的支持子網名稱欄位。 未選中—追蹤結果中將不包含容器圖徵和對象的內容。 在更新子網操作期間，將不會更新內容圖徵的支持子網名稱。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　include_structures

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 包括結構 （可選）
- 指定追蹤結果中是否包含結構圖徵和對象。 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 選中—追蹤結果中將包含結構圖徵和對象。 在更新子網操作期間，將更新與追蹤返回的附件相關聯的結構網絡圖徵的受支持子網名稱欄位。 未選中—追蹤結果中將不包含結構圖徵和對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-31

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　validate_locatability

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 驗證可定位性 （可選）
- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含或附件關聯，是否在追蹤或更新子網操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象的關聯來定位追蹤或更新子網操作返回的非空間對象。 注: 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 選中—如果遇到無法定位的非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含或附件關聯，則將返回一條錯誤。 未選中—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

- 指定輸入層是否支持不相交子網。 不相交子網是屬于同一個層的兩個或多個子網，具有相同的子網名稱，但不可遍歷。 此參數僅適用于具有分區層定義的域網絡內的層。 默認情況下，對于具有等級層定義的域網絡中的層，此參數將設置為 SUPPORT_DISJOINT，以支持不相交子網。 SUPPORT_DISJOINT—輸入層將支持不相交子網。 NO_DISJOINT—輸入層將不支持不相交子網。 除非另有說明，否則此為默認設置。
- Boolean

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-43

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　summaries

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- summaries [[Function, Attribute, Filter Name, Filter Operator, Filter Type, Filter Value, Summary Attribute],...] （可選）
- 設置“匯總屬性”欄位以在插入或更新 SubnetLine 圖徵時存儲函數結果。 可以針對子網中的所有圖徵計算匯總資料。 可以過濾計算以僅應用于具有特定屬性值或類別的網絡圖徵。 這是基于指定函數的匯總屬性欄位的有效欄位類型矩陣。 值表列： Function—計算函數。 MIN—將識別輸入值的最小值。 MAX—將識別輸入值的最大值。 AVERAGE—將計算輸入值的平均值。 COUNT—將識別圖徵數。 ADD—將計算輸入值的總和。 SUBTRACT—將計算輸入值的差異。 子網控制器和循環追蹤類型不支持減法函數。 Attribute—按網絡屬性過濾。 name—按屬性名稱過濾。 operator—按運算符過濾。 IS_EQUAL_TO—屬性與值相等。 DOES_NOT_EQUAL—屬性與值不相等。 IS_GREATER_THAN—屬性大于值。 IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性大于或等于值。 IS_LESS_THAN—屬性小于值。 IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性小于或等于值。 INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == 值）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—并非值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 != 值）。 INCLUDES_ANY—值中至少有一位存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == True）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—值中的所有位均未存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == False）。 type—按屬性類型過濾。 SPECIFIC_VALUE—按特定值過濾。 NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—按網絡屬性過濾。 Filter Value—按特定值過濾。 Summary Attribute—將保留函數結果的欄位。
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-44

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- condition_barriers [[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙根據網絡屬性或類別、操作符、類型及屬性值來定義。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于特定值 Open 時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用 組合方法參數來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 條件障礙組件如下： Name—可按系統中定義的任意網絡屬性進行過濾，或指定 Category 作為網絡類別。 Operator—從大量運算符中進行選擇。 Type—指定 Name 參數的值類型“特定值”或“網絡屬性”，以此作為障礙。 當 Name 參數值為 Category 時，Type 參數值必須為“特定值”。 Value—提供會基于運算符值導致終止的輸入屬性或類別的特定值。 “組合方法”- 如果要添加多個條件，則設置此值。您可以使用 And 或 Or 條件來對它們進行組合。 條件障礙 Operator 值如下： 了解有關使用多條件表達式的詳細信息 值表列： Name—按網絡屬性名稱過濾。 Operator—按運算符過濾。 IS_EQUAL_TO—屬性與值相等。 DOES_NOT_EQUAL—屬性與值不相等。 IS_GREATER_THAN—屬性大于值。 IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性大于或等于值。 IS_LESS_THAN—屬性小于值。 IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性小于或等于值。 INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == 值）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—并非值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 != 值）。 INCLUDES_ANY—值中至少有一位存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == True）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—值中的所有位均未存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == False）。 Type—按特定類型過濾。 SPECIFIC_VALUE—按特定值或網絡類別過濾。 NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—按網絡屬性過濾。 Value—按特定值過濾。 Combine Using—使用此參數可合并多個條件障礙。 AND—合并條件障礙。 OR—滿足任一條件障礙時使用。
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-45

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- function_barriers [[Function, Attribute, Operator, Value, Use Local Values],...] （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 函數障礙組件如下： Function—從大量計算函數中進行選擇。 Attribute—按系統中定義的任何網絡屬性進行過濾。 Operator—從大量運算符中進行選擇。 Value—提供將導致終止的輸入屬性類型（若發現）的特定值。 “使用局部值”- 計算每個方向的值，而不是整體全局值。 例如，用于計算形狀長度總和的函數障礙，其中，如果值大于或等于 4，則追蹤終止。 在全局情況下，遍歷兩條值為 2 的邊之后，形狀長度總和即已達到 4，因此追蹤會停止。 如果使用局部值，每條路徑上的局部值會變化，因此追蹤會持續得更遠。 函數障礙 Function 值選項如下： 值表列： Function—按函數過濾。 MIN—將識別輸入值的最小值。 MAX—將識別輸入值的最大值。 AVERAGE—將計算輸入值的平均值。 COUNT—將識別圖徵數。 ADD—將計算輸入值的總和。 SUBTRACT—將計算輸入值的差異。 子網控制器和循環追蹤類型不支持減法函數。 Attribute—按網絡屬性過濾。 Operator—按運算符過濾。 IS_EQUAL_TO—屬性與值相等。 DOES_NOT_EQUAL—屬性與值不相等。 IS_GREATER_THAN—屬性大于值。 IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性大于或等于值。 IS_LESS_THAN—屬性小于值。 IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—屬性小于或等于值。 INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == 值）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—并非值中的所有位都存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 != 值）。 INCLUDES_ANY—值中至少有一位存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == True）。 DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—值中的所有位均未存在于屬性中的“按位與”運算（按位與 == False）。 Value—按特定值過濾。 Use Local Values—布爾值使用本地變量而不是全局變量。
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-47

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- traversability_scope （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于確定是否在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Open 并將遍歷范圍僅設置為邊，則即使追蹤遇到開路設備，追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 JUNCTIONS_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 EDGES_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**values**　
- propagators [[Attribute, Substitution Attribute, Function, Operator, Value, Propagated Attribute],...] （可選）
- 指定在傳統域網絡中的追蹤過程中，需要傳播的網絡屬性及其傳播方式。 傳播的類屬性表示子網控制器上已傳播至子網余下圖徵的關鍵值。 例如，在配電模型中，您可傳播相位值。 傳播程序組件如下： Attribute—按系統中定義的任何網絡屬性進行過濾。 “替換屬性”- 使用替換值而不是 bitset 網絡屬性值。 替換是基于正在傳播的網絡屬性中的位數進行編碼的。 替換是指同相位的某個位到另一個位的映射。 例如對于相 AC 而言，可通過一個替換將位 A 映射到 B，將位 C 映射到 null。 在該示例中，1010（相 AC）的替換是 0000-0010-0000-0000 (512)。 該替換將捕捉映射，以通知您相 A 被映射到 B，且相 C 被映射到 null 而非相反（即相 A 未映射到 null，且相 C 未映射到 B）。 Function—從大量計算函數中進行選擇。 Operator—從大量運算符中進行選擇。 Value—提供會基于運算符值導致終止的輸入屬性類型的特定值。 “傳播的屬性”- 網絡類中用于存儲所計算傳播值的欄位的名稱。 欄位類型應該與為 Attribute 值指定的網絡屬性的欄位類型相同。 傳播程序 Function 值選項如下： 了解有關按位運算符的詳細信息 值表列： Attribute—按網絡屬性過濾。 Substitution Attribute—用于屬性替換的欄位。 Function—按函數過濾。 Operator—按運算符過濾。 Value—按特定值過濾。 Propagated Attribute—網絡類中用于存儲所計算傳播值的欄位的名稱。
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-61

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**parameter**　validate_locatability

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- validate_locatability （可選）
- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含或附件關聯，是否在追蹤或更新子網操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象的關聯來定位追蹤或更新子網操作返回的非空間對象。 注: 此參數需要公共設施網絡版本值為 5 或更高。 VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—如果遇到無法定位的非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含或附件關聯，則將返回一條錯誤。 DO_NOT_VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-64

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        SetSubnetworkDefinition.py
Description: This script sets the subnetwork definition for a tier in a Utility Network
Created by:  Esri
****************************************************************************'''

# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
in_utility_network = "ElectricDistribution"
domain_network = "ElecDist"
tier_name = "Medium Voltage"
support_disjoint_subnetwork = "SUPPORT_DISJOINT"
valid_devices = "'CircuitBreaker/Unknown';'CircuitBreaker/Air powered';'NetworkProtector/Unknown';'NetworkProtector/NetworkProtector';'Transformer/Unknown';'Transformer/Buck boost';'Transformer/Single-phase overhead';'Transformer/Single-phase padmounted';'Transformer/Three-phase padmounted';'Transformer/Power';'Transformer/Step';'Transformer/Vault'"
valid_subnetwork_controller = "'CircuitBreaker/Unknown';'CircuitBreaker/Air powered'"
valid_lines = "'Busbar/Unknown';'Busbar/Busbar';'Connector/Unknown';'Connector/Connector';'LowVoltage/Unknown';'LowVoltage/Overhead';'LowVoltage/Underground';'MediumVoltage/Unknown';'MediumVoltage/Single-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Two-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Three-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Single-phase underground';'MediumVoltage/Two-phase underground';'MediumVoltage/Three-phase underground';'IsolatedNeutral/Unknown';'IsolatedNeutral/Concentric neutral';'IsolatedNeutral/Neutral';'SubTransmission/Unknown';'SubTransmission/Overhead';'SubTransmission/Underground'"
aggregated_line = "'Busbar/Unknown';'Busbar/Busbar';'MediumVoltage/Unknown';'MediumVoltage/Single-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Two-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Three-phase overhead';'MediumVoltage/Single-phase underground';'MediumVoltage/Two-phase underground';'MediumVoltage/Three-phase underground'"
diagram_template = "Basic"
summaries = ""
condition_barriers = "'Device Status' IS_EQUAL_TO SPECIFIC_VALUE 'Opened' #"
function_barriers = ""
include_barriers = "INCLUDE_BARRIERS"
traversability_scope = "BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES"
propagators = ""

# Run the SetSubnetworkDefinition tool
arcpy.un.SetSubnetworkDefinition(in_utility_network,
                                 domain_network,
                                 tier_name,
                                 support_disjoint_subnetwork,
                                 valid_devices,
                                 valid_subnetwork_controller,
                                 valid_lines,
                                 aggregated_line,
                                 diagram_template,
                                 summaries,
                                 condition_barriers,
                                 function_barriers,
                                 include_barriers,
                                 traversability_scope,
                                 propagators)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#derived-output

## 設置子網定義 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**english**　Set Subnetwork Definition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html

**id**　1b61f646ee8ce6e5e8-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-subnetwork-definition.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Set Subnetwork Definition，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、域網絡 [domain_network]、層名稱 [tier_name]、支持不相交子網 [support_disjoint_subnetwork]、有效設備 （可選） [valid_devices]、有效的設備子網控制器 （可選） [valid_subnetwork_controller]、有效線 （可選） [valid_lines]、子網線圖徵類的聚合線 （可選） [aggregated_line]、子網邏輯示意圖模板 （可選） [diagram_template]、匯總 （可選） [summaries]、條件障礙 （可選） [condition_barriers]、函數障礙 （可選） [function_barriers]、包括障礙圖徵 （可選） [include_barriers]、應用可遍歷性至 （可選） [traversability_scope]、傳播程序 （可選） [propagators]、更新結構網絡容器 （可選） [update_structure_features]、更新域網絡容器 （可選） [update_container_features]、默認版本的編輯模式 （可選） [edit_mode_for_default_version]、指定版本的編輯模式 （可選） [edit_mode_for_named_version]、有效交匯點 （可選） [valid_junctions]、有效交匯點對象 （可選） [valid_junction_objects]、有效交匯點對象子網控制器 （可選） [valid_junction_object_subnetwork_controller]、有效邊對象 （可選） [valid_edge_objects]、管理 IsDirty （可選） [manage_subnetwork_isdirty]、包括容器 （可選） [include_containers]、包括內容 （可選） [include_content]、包括結構 （可選） [include_structures]、驗證可定位性 （可選） [validate_locatability]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

Disjoint subnetworks are two or more subnetworks that belong to the same tier and have the same subnetwork name but are not traversable. This parameter is only available for tiers in domain networks with a partitioned tier definition. This parameter is checked by default for tiers in a domain network with a hierarchical tier definition to support disjoint subnetworks. Checked—The input tier will support disjoint subnetworks. Unchecked—The input tier will not support disjoint subnetworks. This list is a subset of the values specified in the Valid Lines parameter."}, {"parameter": "diagram_template", "label_zh": "子網邏輯示意圖模板 （可選）", "label_en": "Subnetwork Diagram Templates (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The templates that will be used to generate subnetwork system diagrams for each subnetwork."}, {"parameter": "summaries", "label_zh": "匯總 （可選）", "label_en": "Summaries (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets the Summary Attribute field to store function results when inserting or updating SubnetLine features. The summaries can be calculated on all the features in the subnetwork. Calculations can be filtered to apply only to network features with a particular attribute value or category. This is a matrix of valid field types for the summary attribute field based on the specified function. Value table columns: Function—Calculation function. Minimum—The minimum of the input values will be identified. Maximum—The maximum of the input values will be identified. Average—The average of the input values will be calculated. Count—The number of features will be identified. Add—The sum of the input values will be calculated. Subtract—The difference of the input values will be calculated. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Name—Filter by attribute name. Filter Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Filter Type—Filter by attribute type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Value—Filter by a specific value. Summary Attribute—The field that will persist the function result."}, {"parameter": "condition_barriers", "label_zh": "條件障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Condition Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier condition on features based on a comparison to a network attribute or check for a category string. A condition barrier uses a network attribute or network category, an operator and a type, and an attribute value. For example, stop a trace when a feature has the Device Status attribute equal to the specific value of Open. When a feature meets this condition, the trace stops. If you're using more than one attribute, you can use the Combine using parameter to define an And or an Or condition. Condition barrier components are as follows: Name—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system, or select Category to use a network category. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Type—Select Specific value or Network attribute for the type of value from the Name parameter that will serve as a barrier. The Type parameter must be set to Specific value when the Name parameter value is Category. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute or category that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine using—Set this value if you have multiple conditions to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. The condition barriers Operator value options are as follows: Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). The condition barriers Type value options are as follows: Specific Value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value table columns: Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple condition barriers. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met."}, {"parameter": "function_barriers", "label_zh": "函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier on features based on a function. Function barriers can be used to do such things as restrict how far the trace travels from the starting point, or set a maximum value to stop a trace. For example, the length of each line traveled is added to the total distance traveled so far. When the total length traveled reaches the value specified, the trace stops. Function barrier components are as follows: Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value, for example, a function barrier that is calculating the sum of Shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you have traversed two edges with a value of 2, you have already reached a Shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues. Checked—Local values will be used. Unchecked—Global values will be used. This is the default. The function barrier Function value options are as follows: Minimum—The minimum of the input values. Maximum—The maximum of the input values. Add—The sum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Subtract—The difference between the input values. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. For example, the starting point feature has a value of 20. The next feature has a value of 30. If you use the minimum function, the result is 20, maximum is 30, add is 50, average is 25, count is 2, and subtract is -10. The function barrier Operator value options are as follows: Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value table columns: Function—Filter by a function. Minimum—The minimum of the input values will be identified. Maximum—The maximum of the input values will be identified. Average—The average of the input values will be calculated. Count—The number of features will be identified. Add—The sum of the input values will be calculated. Subtract—The difference in the input values will be calculated. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Use local values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable."}, {"parameter": "include_barriers", "label_zh": "包括障礙圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include barrier features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. Traversability barriers are optional even if they have been preset in the subnetwork definition. Checked—Traversability barriers will be included in the trace results. This is the default. Unchecked—Traversability barriers will not be included in the trace results."}, {"parameter": "traversability_scope", "label_zh": "應用可遍歷性至 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Traversability To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of traversability that will be enforced. Traversability scope defines whether traversability will be enforced at junctions, edges, or both. For example, if a condition barrier is defined to stop the trace if Device Status is equal to Open and traversability scope is set to edges only, the trace will not stop—even if it encounters an open device—because Device Status is only applicable to junctions. In other words, this parameter indicates to the trace whether to ignore junctions, edges, or both. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "propagators", "label_zh": "傳播程序 （可選）", "label_en": "Propagators (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Note: This parameter is only available in Python. Value table columns: Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Substitution Attribute—The field that will be used for attribute substitution. Function—Filter by a function. Operator—Filter by an operator. Value—Filter by a specific value. Propagated Attribute—The name of the field in the network class that is used to store the calculated propagated values."}, {"parameter": "update_structure_features", "label_zh": "更新結構網絡容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Update structure network containers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the update subnetwork process will update the supported subnetwork name attribute for structure network containers. Checked—The supported subnetwork name attribute for structure network containers will be updated. This is the default. Unchecked—The structure network containers will not be updated."}, {"parameter": "update_container_features", "label_zh": "更新域網絡容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Update domain network containers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the update subnetwork process will update the supported subnetwork name for domain network containers. Checked—The supported subnetwork name for domain network containers will be updated. This is the default. Unchecked—The domain network containers will not be updated."}, {"parameter": "edit_mode_for_default_version", "label_zh": "默認版本的編輯模式 （可選）", "label_en": "Edit Mode For Default Version (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the edit mode that will be used for subnetwork updates on the default version and with file and mobile geodatabases. Learn more about edit modes set as part of the update subnetwork policy Without eventing—Eventing will not be used for subnetwork updates on the default version or in a file or mobile geodatabase. This edit mode updates the subnetwork name and propagated values in place. This is the default. With eventing—Eventing will be used for subnetwork updates on the default version and in a file or mobile geodatabase. This edit mode applies geodatabase behavior (for example, attribute rules, editor tracking, and so on) when the subnetwork is updated and updates the subnetwork name and propagated values for all applicable features and objects."}, {"parameter": "edit_mode_for_named_version", "label_zh": "指定版本的編輯模式 （可選）", "label_en": "Edit Mode For Named Version (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the edit mode that will be used for subnetwork updates on a named version. Learn more about edit modes set as part of the update subnetwork policy Note: This parameter is only applicable to enterprise geodatabases. Without eventing—Eventing will not be used for subnetwork updates on named versions. This edit mode updates the subnetwork name and propagated values in place for features and objects edited in the version. This is the most performant approach, but subnetwork information does not update on features that were not modified in the version. This is the default. With eventing—Eventing will be used for subnetwork updates on named versions. This edit mode applies geodatabase behavior (for example, attribute rules, editor tracking, and so on) when the subnetwork is updated and updates the subnetwork name and propagated values for all applicable features and objects."}, {"parameter": "valid_junctions", "label_zh": "有效交匯點 （可選）", "label_en": "Valid Junctions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset group/asset type pairs identified as valid junctions for the subnetwork."}, {"parameter": "valid_junction_objects", "label_zh": "有效交匯點對象 （可選）", "label_en": "Valid Junction Objects (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset group/asset type pairs identified as valid junction objects for the subnetwork."}, {"parameter": "valid_junction_object_subnetwork_controller", "label_zh": "有效交匯點對象子網控制器 （可選）", "label_en": "Valid Junction Object Subnetwork Controllers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset group/asset type pairs identified as valid junction object subnetwork controllers for the subnetwork."}, {"parameter": "valid_edge_objects", "label_zh": "有效邊對象 （可選）", "label_en": "Valid Edge Objects (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset group/asset type pairs identified as valid edge objects for the subnetwork."}, {"parameter": "manage_subnetwork_isdirty", "label_zh": "管理 IsDirty （可選）", "label_en": "Manage IsDirty (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the Is dirty attribute in the Subnetworks table will be managed by the validate network topology and update subnetwork operations to update subnetwork status. If no subnetwork controllers are defined for the tier, the parameter remains unchecked by default. Note: This parameter requires a Utility Network Version value of 5 or later. Checked—The Is dirty attribute will be managed by the update subnetwork operation. This is the default except as noted. Unchecked—The Is dirty attribute will not be managed by the update subnetwork operation."}, {"parameter": "include_containers", "label_zh": "包括容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Include containers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the container features and objects will be included in the trace results. Note: This parameter requires a Utility Network Version value of 5 or later. Checked—Container features and objects will be included in the trace results. The supported subnetwork name field on container features will be updated with the subnetwork name of the subnetwork it supports when used in conjunction with the Update structure network containers and Update domain network containers parameters. Unchecked—Container features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_content", "label_zh": "包括內容 （可選）", "label_en": "Include content (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace will include content of containers in the results. Note: This parameter requires a Utility Network Version value of 5 or later. Checked—Content of container features and objects will be included in the trace results. During an update subnetwork operation, the supporting subnetwork name field will be updated for content features returned by the trace. Unchecked—Content of container features and objects will not be included in the trace results. The supporting subnetwork name is not updated for content features during the update subnetwork operation. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_structures", "label_zh": "包括結構 （可選）", "label_en": "Include structures (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Note: This parameter requires a Utility Network Version value of 5 or later. Checked—Structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. During an update subnetwork operation, the supported subnetwork name field will be updated for structure network features associated with attachments returned by the trace. Unchecked—Structure features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "validate_locatability", "label_zh": "驗證可定位性 （可選）", "label_en": "Validate locatability (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an error will be returned during a trace or update subnetwork operation if nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary containment or attachment association in their association hierarchy. This parameter ensures that nonspatial objects returned by a trace or update subnetwork operation can be located through an association with features or other locatable objects. Note: This parameter requires a Utility Network Version value of 5 or later. Checked—An error will be returned if unlocatable nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary containment or attachment association in their association hierarchy. Unchecked—The trace will not check for unlocatable objects and will return results regardless of whether unlocatable objects are present in the association hierarchy of the traversed objects. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.SetSubnetworkDefinition(in_utility_network, domain_network, tier_name, support_disjoint_subnetwork, {valid_devices}, {valid_subnetwork_controller}, {valid_lines}, {aggregated_line}, {diagram_template}, {summaries}, {condition_barriers}, {function_barriers}, {include_barriers}, {traversability_scope}, {propagators}, {update_structure_features}, {update_container_features}, {edit_mode_for_default_version}, {edit_mode_for_named_version}, {valid_junctions}, {valid_junction_objects}, {valid_junction_object_subnetwork_controller}, {valid_edge_objects}, {manage_subnetwork_isdirty}, {include_containers}, {include_content}, {include_structures}, {validate_locatability})

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。

工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 《遠程參考工具》更新措施 · 摘要摘要

**id**　1b72fd35208ffac5d2-1

**english**　Update Measures From LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html

**text**　以任何點或線性特征,包括管道、裝置和接合點等實用網絡特征或事件和交叉點等LRS特征,對路徑和測量屬性進行彈射或更新。將更新的屬性包括路線識別、路線名稱(如果配置了)和計量。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Populates or updates the route and measure attributes on any point or line features, including Utility Network features such as pipes, devices, and junctions, or LRS features such as events and intersections.\nThe attributes that will be updated include route ID, route name (if configured), and measures.", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#summary

## 《遠程參考工具》更新措施 · 使用率

**id**　1b72fd35208ffac5d2-2

**english**　Update Measures From LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html

**text**　此工具支持來自文件地理資料庫、企業地理資料庫(部門版版版連接)或功能服務(由分支版資料發布)的資料。 空間引用、 x,y分辨率和 x, 輸入特征和 LRS 網絡參數值的容度必須匹配。 路徑 ID 欄位和 路線 命名欄位參數是可選的,并且適用于橫跨路徑的特征。 如果輸入特性與路徑不吻合,則使用搜索容忍參數來更新輸入特性的路徑和測量屬性。 搜索耐受度范圍內最近的路徑路徑和測量將用來更新輸入特性。 如果兩個或兩個以上路徑與輸入特性等距, 任何一條都將用于更新。如果輸入特性是一個線性特征,只有當線性特征的開始和結束在路線搜索容度之內時,才會對路徑和測量屬性進行顯示。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "This tool supports data from a file geodatabase, an enterprise geodatabase (branch versioned connection), or a feature service (published from branch versioned data).\nThe spatial reference, x,y resolution, and x,y tolerance of the Input Features and LRS Network parameter values must match.\nThe To Route ID Field and To Route Name Field parameters are optional and are applicable for features that span routes.\nThe Search Tolerance parameter is used to update the route and measure attributes of the input features if they are not coincident with a route. The nearest route's route and measure available within the search tolerance will be used to update the input features. If two or more routes are equidistant from an input feature, any one of those routes will be used for the update. If the input feature is a line feature, the route and measure attributes will be populated only when the start and end of the line feature are within the search tolerance of a route.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#usage

Updated route and measure attributes in the input layer Feature Type Route ID Measure Comments Point Route1 21 The point feature is on Route1. Updated route and measure attributes in the input layer Feature Type Route ID Measure Comments Point Route1 21 The point feature is not on Route1. By providing a search tolerance, the route and measure attributes are updated from the nearest route. Updated route and measure attributes in the input layer Feature Type Route ID Route Name From Measure To Route ID To Route Name To Measure Comments Line Route1 L1R1 11 Route2 L1R2 28 The line feature's geometry has a geometric match with routes L1R1 and L1R2, which belong to the same line. Updated route and measure attributes in the input layer", "section": "Usage"}

## 《遠程參考工具》更新措施 · 代碼樣本

**id**　1b72fd35208ffac5d2-31

**english**　Update Measures From LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html

**text**　# Name: UpdateMeasuresFromLRS_Sample1.py
# Description: This will populate the Route ID and measures in the input layer based on overlapping routes from the network layer.
# The output is the input layer with the updated Route ID and measure values, and a .csv file with information on the updated features and errors.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Variables are supported from feature service only. Signing into Portal is required to access the feature service.
arcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')

# Map the lrs network from the feature service. Here, 8 corresponds to the lrs route network.
lrs_network_url =  r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/8"
lrs_network = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(lrs_network_url, "networkLayer")

# Map the input feature layer from the same feature service. Here, 18 corresponds to the input feature layer.
in_features_url = r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/18"
in_features = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features_url, "inputFeaturesLayer")
lrs_date = "10/8/2019"
route_id_field = "ROUTEID"
from_measure_field = "FROMMEASURE"
to_measure_field = "TOMEASURE"
to_route_id_field = None
route_name_field = None
to_route_name_field = None
search_tolerance = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: UpdateMeasuresFromLRS_Sample1.py\n# Description: This will populate the Route ID and measures in the input layer based on overlapping routes from the network layer.\n# The output is the input layer with the updated Route ID and measure values, and a .csv file with information on the updated features and errors.\n# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Variables are supported from feature service only. Signing into Portal is required to access the feature service.\narcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')\n\n# Map the lrs network from the feature service. Here, 8 corresponds to the lrs route network.\nlrs_network_url =  r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/8\"\nlrs_network = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(lrs_network_url, \"networkLayer\")\n\n# Map the input feature layer from the same feature service. Here, 18 corresponds to the input feature layer.\nin_features_url = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/18\"\nin_features = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features_url, \"inputFeaturesLayer\")\nlrs_date = \"10/8/2019\"\nroute_id_field = \"ROUTEID\"\nfrom_measure_field = \"FROMMEASURE\"\nto_measure_field = \"TOMEASURE\"\nto_route_id_field = None\nroute_name_field = None\nto_route_name_field = None\nsearch_tolerance = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)", "section": "Code sample"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#derived-output

from_measure_field = "FROMMEASURE"
to_measure_field = "TOMEASURE"
to_route_id_field = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)

Portal signin is required to access the feature service.\narcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')\n\n# Initialize variables\n# Map the lrs network from the feature service. Here, 8 corresponds to the LRS route network.\nlrs_network_url =  r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/8\"\nlrs_network = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(lrs_network_url, \"networkLayer\")\n\n# Map the input feature layer from the same feature service. Here, 18 corresponds to the input feature layer.\nin_features_url = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/18\"\nin_features = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features_url, \"inputFeaturesLayer\")\nlrs_date= \"10/8/2019\"\nroute_id_field = \"ROUTEID\"\nfrom_measure_field = \"FROMMEASURE\"\nto_measure_field = \"TOMEASURE\"\nto_route_id_field = None\nroute_name_field = None\nto_route_name_field = None\nsearch_tolerance = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)", "section": "Code sample"}

## 《遠程參考工具》更新措施 · 代碼樣本

**id**　1b72fd35208ffac5d2-35

**english**　Update Measures From LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html

**text**　# Name: UpdateMeasuresFromLRS_sample3.py
# Description: This will populate the Route ID, Route Name, and measures in the input layer based on overlapping routes from the network layer.
# The output is the input layer with the updated Route ID, Route Name and measure values, and a .csv file with information on the updated features and errors.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Variables are supported from feature service only. Signing into Portal is required to access the feature service.
arcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')

# Map the lrs network from the feature service. Here, 8 corresponds to the lrs route network.
lrs_network_url =  r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/8"
lrs_network = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(lrs_network_url, "networkLayer")

# Map the input feature layer from the same feature service. Here, 18 corresponds to the input spannning feature layer.
in_features_url = r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/18"
in_features = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features_url, "inputFeaturesLayer")
lrs_date = "12/31/2024"
route_id_field = "ROUTEID"
from_measure_field = "FROMMEASURE"
to_measure_field = "TOMEASURE"
to_route_id_field = "TOROUTEID"
route_name_field = "RteName"
to_route_name_field = "ToRteName"
search_tolerance = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: UpdateMeasuresFromLRS_sample3.py\n# Description: This will populate the Route ID, Route Name, and measures in the input layer based on overlapping routes from the network layer.\n# The output is the input layer with the updated Route ID, Route Name and measure values, and a .csv file with information on the updated features and errors.\n# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Variables are supported from feature service only. Signing into Portal is required to access the feature service.\narcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')\n\n# Map the lrs network from the feature service. Here, 8 corresponds to the lrs route network.\nlrs_network_url =  r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/8\"\nlrs_network = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(lrs_network_url, \"networkLayer\")\n\n# Map the input feature layer from the same feature service. Here, 18 corresponds to the input spannning feature layer.\nin_features_url = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/18\"\nin_features = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features_url, \"inputFeaturesLayer\")\nlrs_date = \"12/31/2024\"\nroute_id_field = \"ROUTEID\"\nfrom_measure_field = \"FROMMEASURE\"\nto_measure_field = \"TOMEASURE\"\nto_route_id_field = \"TOROUTEID\"\nroute_name_field = \"RteName\"\nto_route_name_field = \"ToRteName\"\nsearch_tolerance = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, to_measure_field, to_route_id_field, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, search_tolerance)", "section": "Code sample"}

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "lrs_network", "label_zh": "LRS 網絡", "label_en": "LRS Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layer that contains the routes, route IDs, route names (if configured), and measures."}, {"parameter": "lrs_date", "label_zh": "LRS 日期", "label_en": "LRS Date", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The date that will be used to define the temporal view of the network."}, {"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入特性", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point or line features that will be updated based on feature geometry relative to the routes in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "route_id_field", "label_zh": "線路ID外地", "label_en": "Route ID Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the route ID attribute. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "from_measure_field", "label_zh": "計量欄位", "label_en": "Measure Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the measure value for point features or the start measure value for line features. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "to_measure_field", "label_zh": "測量欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "To Measure Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the end measure value for line features. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "to_route_id_field", "label_zh": "至路由ID欄位(可選)", "label_en": "To Route ID Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the to route ID attribute. This parameter is only available when the Input Features parameter value is a line feature layer. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "route_name_field", "label_zh": "路線名稱欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Route Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the route name attribute. This parameter is only available when the LRS Network has a route name configured. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "to_route_name_field", "label_zh": "到路線名稱欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "To Route Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the Input Features parameter value that contains the to route name attribute. This parameter is only available when the LRS Network has a route name configured and the Input Features parameter value is a line feature layer. The data type and length of the field must match those of the corresponding field in the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "search_tolerance", "label_zh": "容忍(可選擇)", "label_en": "Search Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The routes that are within the search tolerance of the input feature will be identified. The nearest route's route and measure available within the search tolerance will be used to update the input features. The search tolerance unit is based on the x,y unit of the LRS Network."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.lr.UpdateMeasuresFromLRS(lrs_network, lrs_date, in_features, route_id_field, from_measure_field, {to_measure_field}, {to_route_id_field}, {route_name_field}, {to_route_name_field}, {search_tolerance})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：lrs_network、lrs_date、in_features、route_id_field、from_measure_field、to_measure_field、to_route_id_field、route_name_field、to_route_name_field、search_tolerance。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 《遠程參考工具》更新措施 · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**english**　Update Measures From LRS

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html

**id**　1b72fd35208ffac5d2-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Update Measures From LRS，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。LRS 網絡 [lrs_network]、LRS 日期 [lrs_date]、輸入特性 [in_features]、線路ID外地 [route_id_field]、計量欄位 [from_measure_field]、測量欄位( 可選) [to_measure_field]、至路由ID欄位(可選) [to_route_id_field]、路線名稱欄位( 可選) [route_name_field]、到路線名稱欄位( 可選) [to_route_name_field]、容忍(可選擇) [search_tolerance]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/update-measures-from-lrs.html#parameters

## 圖徵轉網格 (網格分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1b8804dc0946b787c0-2

**english**　Convert Feature To Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網格分析工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html

**text**　如果您登錄到具有針對網格分析進行了配置 ArcGIS Image Server 的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶，則網格分析門戶工具可用。 當工具運行時，ArcGIS Pro 將充當客戶端，并且處理將在與 ArcGIS Enterprise 聯合的服務器中進行。 門戶工具接受來自門戶的圖層作為輸入，并在門戶中創建輸出。 輸入圖徵圖層可以是門戶中的圖層。 它也可以是圖徵服務的 URI 或 URL。 此工具不支持本地圖徵資料和圖層作為輸入。
點、線或面圖徵可以轉換為網格資料集。

## 圖徵轉網格 (網格分析工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　1b8804dc0946b787c0-18

**english**　Convert Feature To Raster

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網格分析工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html

**text**　#-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Name: ConvertFeatureToRaster_Ex_02.py
# Description: Convert point features to a raster.
#
# Requirements: ArcGIS Image Server

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
inFeatures = 'https://MyPortal.esri.com/server/rest/services/Hosted/Rainfall/FeatureServer/0'
inField = 'elevation'
outCellSize = '250 Meters'
outRaster = 'outRaster'

# Run ConvertFeatureToRaster
arcpy.ra.ConvertFeatureToRaster(inFeatures, inField, outRaster, outCellSize)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html#derived-output

## 圖徵轉網格 (網格分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網格分析工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**english**　Convert Feature To Raster

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html

**id**　1b8804dc0946b787c0-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Convert Feature To Raster，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [inputFeatures]、值欄位 [valueField]、輸出名稱 [outputName]、輸出像元大小 [outputCellSize]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/raster-analysis/convert-feature-to-raster.html#parameters

This parameter only supports numeric field types."}, {"parameter": "outputName", "label_zh": "輸出名稱", "label_en": "Output Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output raster service. If the layer name already exists, you will be prompted to provide another name."}, {"parameter": "outputCellSize", "label_zh": "輸出像元大小", "label_en": "Output Cell Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "Enter the cell size and unit for the output raster. **signatures**

## 計算深度地圖 (實景映射工具) · 匯總

**id**　1b96ff8e9113f5c528-1

**english**　Compute Depth Map

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 實景映射工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html

**text**　根據深度地圖為組成鑲嵌資料集的每個圖像計算更準確的 CenterZ 欄位值。 控制點和解決方案點用于為組成鑲嵌資料集的每個圖像計算深度地圖，以改善圖像到地面（地圖）的變換，尤其在傾斜角較高的情況下。
圖像檢查通常在圖像空間中執行，以便發現缺陷、執行測量，并為校正后的影像生成檢查報告。 可以在地圖空間或圖像空間中測量對象的距離、面積和高度，并且可以生成檢查報告以共享檢查結果。
檢查工作流的一個重要組成部分是從圖像到地面（地圖）空間的變換，以便更加精確地定位和測量圖像上的缺陷、點、線、面。 圖像到地面的變換（特別是對于高傾斜圖像）將使用深度地圖，即每個像素從相機位置到地面位置的距離。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#summary

## 計算深度地圖 (實景映射工具) · 用法

**id**　1b96ff8e9113f5c528-2

**english**　Compute Depth Map

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 實景映射工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html

**text**　該工具將經過校正的鑲嵌資料集、控制點表和解決方案點表作為輸入，以生成更新的已校正鑲嵌資料集。 注: 將為包含已校正鑲嵌資料集的每個輸入圖像項目創建深度地圖文件 (.dm)。 將在與包含原始已校正鑲嵌資料集的源影像文件相同的目錄中創建深度地圖文件。
更新的已校正鑲嵌資料集中的 CenterZ 欄位將更新為更加精確的值。
此工具中使用的控制點表和解決方案點表是在使用正射映射工作流或 ArcGIS Reality for ArcGIS Pro 執行區域網平差時生成的。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#usage

**parameter**　skip_existing

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 跳過現有的 （可選）
- 指定是僅針對沒有 CenterZ 值的網格計算深度地圖 CenterZ 值，還是針對所有鑲嵌資料集項目（包括具有現有 CenterZ 值的網格項目）計算深度地圖值。 選中—僅針對沒有 CenterZ 值的網格計算深度地圖 CenterZ 值。 未選中—將為每個鑲嵌資料集項目（包括具有現有 CenterZ 值的項目）計算深度地圖 CenterZ 值。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**values**　
- adjust_footprints （可選）
- 指定是否使用應用于圖像的相同變換來更新輪廓線幾何。 ADJUST_FOOTPRINTS—輪廓線幾何將更新為圖像幾何。 NO_ADJUST_FOOTPRINTS—將不會更新輪廓線幾何。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

## 計算深度地圖 (實景映射工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　1b96ff8e9113f5c528-22

**english**　Compute Depth Map

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 實景映射工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Define input parameters
in_mosaic_dataset= r"C:\CDM_RM.gdb\YVWD"
control_point_table=r"C:\CDM_RM.gdb\YVWD_ControlPoints"
solution_point_table= r"C:\CDM_RM.gdb\YVWD_SolutionPoints",
where_clause= "OBJECTID > 2",
skip_existing="SKIP_EXISTING",
adjust_footprints="ADJUST_FOOTPRINTS"

# Run
arcpy.rm.ComputeDepthMap(in_mosaic_dataset, control_point_table, solution_point_table, where_clause, skip_existing, adjust_footprints)

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 計算深度地圖 (實景映射工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 實景映射工具箱

**english**　Compute Depth Map

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html

**id**　1b96ff8e9113f5c528-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Compute Depth Map，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入鑲嵌資料集 [in_mosaic_dataset]、控制點表 [control_point_table]、解決方案點表 [solution_point_table]、查詢定義 （可選） [where_clause]、跳過現有的 （可選） [skip_existing]、調整輪廓線 （可選） [adjust_footprints]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/reality-mapping/compute-depth-map.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_mosaic_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入鑲嵌資料集", "label_en": "Input Mosaic Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Mosaic Dataset; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The block adjusted input mosaic dataset. The mosaic dataset must be adjusted before it is used as input for this tool. You can use an ortho mapping workflow in ArcGIS Pro, or a Reality for ArcGIS Pro workflow, to adjust the mosaic dataset."}, {"parameter": "control_point_table", "label_zh": "控制點表", "label_en": "Control Point Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class; Table View", "description_en": "The input control point feature class. This point feature class is the output of the Compute Camera Model tool or the Compute Tie Points tool."}, {"parameter": "solution_point_table", "label_zh": "解決方案點表", "label_en": "Solution Point Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class; Table View", "description_en": "The input solution point feature class. This point feature class is the output of the Compute Camera Model tool or the Compute Tie Points tool."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "查詢定義 （可選）", "label_en": "Query Definition (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that will be used to select items in the mosaic dataset to include in the depth map."}, {"parameter": "skip_existing", "label_zh": "跳過現有的 （可選）", "label_en": "Skip Existing (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a depth map CenterZ value will be computed only for rasters without a CenterZ value, or computed for all mosaic dataset items including those with an existing CenterZ value. Checked—A depth map CenterZ value will only be computed for rasters that do not have a CenterZ value. Unchecked—A depth map CenterZ value will be computed for every mosaic dataset item, including items with an existing CenterZ value. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "adjust_footprints", "label_zh": "調整輪廓線 （可選）", "label_en": "Adjust Footprints (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the footprint geometry will be updated using the same transformation that was applied to the image. Checked—The footprint geometry will be updated to the image geometry. Unchecked—The footprint geometry will not be updated. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.rm.ComputeDepthMap(in_mosaic_dataset, control_point_table, solution_point_table, {where_clause}, {skip_existing}, {adjust_footprints})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_mosaic_dataset、control_point_table、solution_point_table、where_clause、skip_existing、adjust_footprints。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 清除工作空間緩存 (資料管理工具) · 注:

**id**　1bb84ae3c8975c172a-2

**english**　Clear Workspace Cache

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 工作空間工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html

**text**　地理處理框架使用工作空間緩存來提高運行腳本工具或重復進程時的性能。 工作空間緩存可以保存給定工作空間的方案鎖，并且可以清除緩存以允許其他進程或功能繼續進行。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html#summary

## 清除工作空間緩存 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　1bb84ae3c8975c172a-3

**english**　Clear Workspace Cache

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 工作空間工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html

**text**　可選單個輸入參數。 如果在未指定工作空間的情況下運行該工具，工作空間緩存中的所有內容都將被清除。 要刪除有關特定工作空間的緩存信息，請使用該地理資料庫、文件夾或 .sde 連接文件作為輸入。
在長時間運行的應用程序中，可以使用此工具斷開空閑的企業級地理資料庫連接。
要清除 Python 腳本中的工作空間緩存，請使用該工具作為腳本中的最終調用。 在調用此工具之前，使用 Python del 語句刪除對可能指向工作空間的對象或變量的所有引用。 如果腳本同時使用 ArcPy 命令和其他 Python 文件和文件夾管理命令，則工作空間緩存持有的方案鎖可能會阻止其他 Python 命令成功運行。 清除工作空間緩存可以成功地刪除或修改文件或文件夾這樣的功能，并且不會被方案鎖阻止。
ArcGIS Pro 或獨立 Python 進程中使用的前十個工作空間將包含在工作空間緩存中。 同一進程中使用的其他工作空間將不會被緩存。 注: 清除企業級地理資料庫工作空間緩存中的工作空間時，不能保證會斷開與企業級地理資料庫服務器的連接。 只能確保 ArcGIS 系統不再對已清除的企業級地理資料庫工作空間有任何保留。 如果任何其他進程含有到此企業級地理資料庫工作空間的引用，則將維持兩者的連接。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html#usage

## 清除工作空間緩存 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 工作空間工具集

**english**　Clear Workspace Cache

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html

**id**　1bb84ae3c8975c172a-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Clear Workspace Cache，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入工作空間 （可選） [in_data]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/clear-workspace-cache.html#parameters

## 面插值圖層到面 (地統計分析工具) · 用法

**id**　1bcced55aaf6677adb-2

**english**　Areal Interpolation Layer To Polygons

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 使用地統計圖層

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html

**text**　此工具用于重新聚合面資料。 在地統計向導中創建面插值圖層后，此工具會將預測結果聚合至一組新的面。
此工具可在工作流用于對面資料進行降尺度或升尺度處理，例如根據郵政編碼中的人口計數預測普查區塊中的人口。
輸入地統計圖層必須是對資料集執行面插值的結果。 此工具不支持通過其它插值方法生成的地統計圖層。
輸出圖徵類中的欄位可能包括以下內容（如適用）： Predicted—面的預測值。 預測值的解釋取決于用于創建面插值地統計圖層的資料類型： 平均值（高斯）—預測面中高斯變量的平均值。 比率（二項式）—預測具有特定特征的面人口的比例。 事件（過度離散泊松）—預測面中的計數。 如果指定了時間欄位，工具預測單位時間的計數。 StdError—面中預測值的標準誤差。 Included—表示是否可以對該面做出穩定預測。 如果無法做出預測，描述中將說明無法預測的原因。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#usage

## 面插值圖層到面 (地統計分析工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　1bcced55aaf6677adb-16

**english**　Areal Interpolation Layer To Polygons

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 使用地統計圖層

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html

**text**　# Name: ArealInterpolationLayerToPolygons_Example_02.py
# Description: Averages (in the case of Gaussian data) or aggregates (in the cases of Binomial or Poisson)
#    the predictions of an Areal Interpolation layer to a new set of polygons.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inArealInterpolationLayer = "C:/gapyexamples/data/AI_layer.lyr"
inPolygonFeatures = "C:/gapyexamples/data/new_polys.shp"
outFeatureClass = "C:/gapyexamples/output/aiout.shp"
appendAllFields = "FID_ONLY"

# Run ArealInterpolationLayerToPolygons
arcpy.ga.ArealInterpolationLayerToPolygons(inArealInterpolationLayer, inPolygonFeatures, outFeatureClass, appendAllFields)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#parameters

## 面插值圖層到面 (地統計分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 使用地統計圖層

**english**　Areal Interpolation Layer To Polygons

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html

**id**　1bcced55aaf6677adb-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Areal Interpolation Layer To Polygons，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入面插值地統計圖層 [in_areal_interpolation_layer]、輸入面圖徵 [in_polygon_features]、輸出面圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、從輸入圖徵追加所有欄位 （可選） [append_all_fields]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/areal-interpolation-layer-to-polygons.html#parameters

## 更改網格資料集的地理座標：地理配準 · 更改網格資料集的地理座標：地理配準

**id**　1bd838567aff3e3abf-1

**english**　Change the geographic coordinates of a raster dataset: Georeferencing

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 投影和變換工具集
- 投影和變換工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/changing-the-geographic-coords-of-a-raster.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/changing-the-geographic-coords-of-a-raster.html

**text**　一般通過以下方法獲取網格資料：掃描地圖、收集航空攝影以及獲取原始衛星影像。 已掃描的地圖資料集通常不包含有關該區域在地球表面上的位置的信息（無論是嵌入在切片中，還是作為單獨的文件）。 原始航空照片和衛星影像提供的位置信息通常不足，無法執行分析或者與其他資料精確對齊顯示。 要將這些類型的網格資料與空間資料結合使用，通常需要將其與地圖座標系對齊或者進行地理配準。
對網格資料集進行地理配準時，可以使用地圖座標來定義資料的位置。 通過將起點鏈接到終點來指定地理配準。 此過程包括分配座標系，用于關聯資料與地球上的特定位置。 通過對網格資料進行地理配準，可將網格資料與其他地理資料一起查看、查詢和分析。
下圖顯示了如何對兩個不重疊的資料集進行地理配準（使用鏈接）以創建兩個重疊的資料集。 當資料集重疊后，其對于分析和顯示將更加有用。
對圖像進行地理配準的另一種方法是平移圖像。 如果網格資料的方向和比例正確，但需要將其平移至新地理位置，則平移圖像將非常有用。 下圖顯示了經過平移的網格資料集的示例。 第一個圖像顯示了街道折線與網格圖像未正確對齊。 第二個圖像顯示了經過平移的網格資料集，以便與街道正確對齊。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/changing-the-geographic-coords-of-a-raster.html

**text**　構建 ArcGIS 應用程序時假設圖徵的幾何遵循特定規范。 如果處理算法遇到不遵循規范的資料，軟件可能會運行不佳、生成錯誤或崩潰；或者操作在無任何明顯問題下成功運行，但結果可能不準確。

## 用于檢查和修復幾何的工具 · 無效幾何的起點

**id**　1bda7a0c9e0f3f08df-2

**english**　Tools for checking and repairing geometries

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 圖徵工具集
- 圖徵工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/checking-and-repairing-geometries.html#B58

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/checking-and-repairing-geometries.html

**text**　shapefile 是帶有文檔格式規范的開放格式。 許多軟件包支持寫入這個格式。 但是，某些軟件包可能由于存在漏洞或缺失相關信息而無法遵循文檔格式的規范。
將 shapefile 加載到地理資料庫時，圖徵的幾何將原樣（包含現有幾何問題）復制，因此使用地理資料庫圖徵類必須同使用 shapefile 資料一樣考慮采取相同的預防措施。 使用 ArcGIS 客戶端應用程序將資料加載到企業級地理資料庫屬于例外情況。 地理處理工具（包括，但不限于 導出圖徵類 、 圖徵類到地理資料庫 以及 追加 ）將檢查和修復圖徵的幾何，以此作為加載過程的一部分。
DBMS 系統提供原生幾何欄位類型。 這些欄位類型可以通過手動 SQL 表達式或從其他軟件包上傳和修改。 這些系統可能創建對 ArcGIS 無效的幾何。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/checking-and-repairing-geometries.html#B58

## 110509：輸入熱點結果必須至少包含 20 個圖徵交叉點。 · 描述

**id**　1bf079e75d56dacd38-1

**english**　110509: The input hot spot results must have at least 20 feature intersections.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110501-110600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html

**text**　輸入熱點結果圖層會在分析之前相交，從而使每個結果圖層中的每個圖徵都可與其他結果圖層的單個圖徵進行配對。 所提供的圖層并沒有至少 20 個圖徵交叉點，此為必要條件。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html#description

## 110509：輸入熱點結果必須至少包含 20 個圖徵交叉點。 · 解決方案

**id**　1bf079e75d56dacd38-2

**english**　110509: The input hot spot results must have at least 20 feature intersections.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110501-110600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html

**text**　對于面結果圖層，請驗證面中是否有足夠的重疊，以保證至少存在 20 個交叉點。
對于點，請驗證每個結果中至少有 20 個點共享相同的座標。
對于線，請驗證每個結果中至少有 20 條線彼此交叉或共享多個折點。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110509.html#solution
